[13:19:47 - 13:19:57] I showed them that there were ways, if they were trying to detect the magic, then if somebody
[13:19:57 - 13:20:03] tried to sneak the normal way, they would be left surprised.
[13:20:03 - 13:20:11] If they relied too much on just magic, they would be flat-footed when someone was trained
[13:20:11 - 13:20:22] mostly like Tom was, and I suppose I was given some level of responsibility because of that.
[13:20:22 - 13:20:29] Alright, he says, we have a plan here, sabi niya, do you continue on with the complaint
[13:20:29 - 13:20:37] with the Peerage, sabi niya, how can I continue to do that after?
[13:20:37 - 13:20:42] Because we gotta show who, we gotta know who are the traitors.
[13:20:42 - 13:20:49] Wouldn't they know that he has a ring, a new ring that's on this finger?
[13:20:49 - 13:20:55] Wouldn't these parasites?
[13:20:55 - 13:21:00] If we remove the parasite, they will be notified that he died.
[13:21:00 - 13:21:04] That's how you learned that the Duchess had died, right?
[13:21:04 - 13:21:09] Now I think I know what the Duchess is saying.
[13:21:09 - 13:21:17] How do we know that this thing, who's not accessing his brain anymore, isn't like screaming at the
[13:21:17 - 13:21:21] others right now, "Hey, help me, help me, help me!"
[13:21:21 - 13:21:23] Yeah, it can't, because of the Ring of Mind Shielding.
[13:21:23 - 13:21:28] Well, it's shielding his mind, but is it shielding it?
[13:21:28 - 13:21:32] The thing is inside his mind, it's like part of the brain.
[13:21:32 - 13:21:34] Alright, so it's trapping him in there?
[13:21:34 - 13:21:35] Yep.
[13:21:35 - 13:21:40] Well, I hope that's the case, sabi niya.
[13:21:40 - 13:21:53] So you want your parasite to be out, and get away from all of this, because it'll ruin someone else's day if he does not, she says.
[13:21:53 - 13:21:57] We gotta continue on this facade for now, she says.
[13:21:57 - 13:22:02] I want to know what it is that I am to do.
[13:22:02 - 13:22:14] If I, like I told you what I told them, if I'm going to go all in, I would like to hear everything.
[13:22:14 - 13:22:16] I would like to know what the plan is.
[13:22:16 - 13:22:21] I'm not going to sit just following orders quietly.
[13:22:21 - 13:22:23] I'm not ordering you, she says.
[13:22:23 - 13:22:25] I ask you what you want to do.
[13:22:25 - 13:22:27] That is what I want to do.
[13:22:27 - 13:22:29] Alright.
[13:22:29 - 13:22:31] Sabi niya.
[13:22:31 - 13:22:35] Whether I agree to this or not will be dependent on that.
[13:22:35 - 13:22:44] Give us the whole night, she says, until the morning, until the dusk, until the dawn breaks.
[13:22:44 - 13:22:49] We will give you information about Matthew, if we save her.
[13:22:49 - 13:22:52] We're giving time for our people inside the Travertine ward.
[13:22:52 - 13:22:54] Sabi niya.
[13:22:54 - 13:22:59] But I will go there, she says.
[13:22:59 - 13:23:04] I will use a, I will teleport to your son, she says.
[13:23:04 - 13:23:05] Take him out there, she says.
[13:23:05 - 13:23:09] Sabi niya.
[13:23:09 - 13:23:13] The plan is tonight, you continue on your rallying.
